FROM "jobPosts" AS j
INNER JOIN recruiters AS r ON j."RecruiterID" = r.guid
INNER JOIN companies AS c ON r."CompanyId" = c.guid
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p.guid, p."JobPostId", p."SkillId", s.guid AS guid0, s."Name"
    FROM "postSkills" AS p
    INNER JOIN skills AS s ON p."SkillId" = s.guid
) AS s3 ON j.guid = s3."JobPostId"
LEFT JOIN responsibilities AS r0 ON j.guid = r0."PostId"
LEFT JOIN locations AS l ON j.guid = l."JobPostId"
LEFT JOIN requirements AS r1 ON j.guid = r1."PostId"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT j0.guid, j0."AppliedOn", j0."IsDeleted", j0."JobPostId", j0."SeekerId", j0."Status", s0.guid AS guid0, s0."About", s0."ConnectionId", s0."DateOfBirth", s0."FirstName", s0."Gender", s0."IsDeleted" AS "IsDeleted0", s0."LastName", s0."UserId", e.guid AS guid1, e."SeekerId" AS "SeekerId0", e."collegeName", e."courseName", e.grade, e0."Guid" AS "Guid2", e0."CompanyName", e0."Description", e0."FromMonth", e0."FromYear", e0."Role", e0."SeekerId" AS "SeekerId1", e0."ToMonth", e0."ToYear", s4.guid AS guid3, s4."SeekerId" AS "SeekerId2", s4."SkillId", s4.guid0 AS guid00, s4."Name"
    FROM "jobApplications" AS j0
    INNER JOIN seekers AS s0 ON j0."SeekerId" = s0.guid
    LEFT JOIN educations AS e ON s0.guid = e."SeekerId"
    LEFT JOIN experiences AS e0 ON s0.guid = e0."SeekerId"
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT s1.guid, s1."SeekerId", s1."SkillId", s2.guid AS guid0, s2."Name"
        FROM "seekerSkills" AS s1
        INNER JOIN skills AS s2 ON s1."SkillId" = s2.guid
    ) AS s4 ON s0.guid = s4."SeekerId"
) AS s5 ON j.guid = s5."JobPostId"
WHERE NOT (j."IsDeleted")
ORDER BY j.guid, r.guid, c.guid, s3.guid, s3.guid0, r0.guid, l.guid, r1.guid, s5.guid, s5.guid0, s5.guid1, s5."Guid2", s5.guid3

FROM "jobPosts" AS j
INNER JOIN recruiters AS r ON j."RecruiterID" = r.guid
INNER JOIN companies AS c ON r."CompanyId" = c.guid
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p.guid, p."JobPostId", p."SkillId", s.guid AS guid0, s."Name"
    FROM "postSkills" AS p
    INNER JOIN skills AS s ON p."SkillId" = s.guid
) AS s3 ON j.guid = s3."JobPostId"
LEFT JOIN responsibilities AS r0 ON j.guid = r0."PostId"
LEFT JOIN locations AS l ON j.guid = l."JobPostId"

FROM "jobPosts" AS j
INNER JOIN recruiters AS r ON j."RecruiterID" = r.guid
INNER JOIN companies AS c ON r."CompanyId" = c.guid
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT p.guid, p."JobPostId", p."SkillId", s.guid AS guid0, s."Name"
    FROM "postSkills" AS p
    INNER JOIN skills AS s ON p."SkillId" = s.guid
) AS s3 ON j.guid = s3."JobPostId"

LEFT JOIN locations AS l ON j.guid = l."JobPostId"
